## Corpus Check
- 1146 files · ~968,633 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 8018 nodes · 13609 edges · 647 communities (590 shown, 57 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 86% EXTRACTED · 14% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1902 edges (avg confidence: 0.95)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e1678554`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
